placement depends on body-to-EEF offset\\n- [After releasing into a lined basket, retreat straight up before moving laterally](global/retreat-high-before-lateral-near-lined-basket.md) \\u2014 placing items into a woven/cloth-lined basket or any container with a soft rim/liner that stands above the rigid rim; especially multi-item drop-ins where you must traverse away between placements\\n- [Clear look-alike cans by RGB label, never by SAM3 brand-noun prompt](global/sam3-brand-noun-can-collision.md) \\u2014 two or more similar cans/cylinders are present and the task names one by brand (\\\"tomato sauce\\\", \\\"alphabet soup\\\", a soup can)\\n- [Re-issue a pick with a nearby landmark when generic picking makes only contact.](global/spatial-qualified-repick.md) \\u2014 A generic Pi0 pick contacts the target object but leaves it upright or ungrasped, and a distinctive nearby landmark is visible in the scene.\\n- [Support an elongated held object on the destination floor before chaining yaw and pitch rotations](global/support-assisted-axis-reorientation.md) \\u2014 an elongated held object must be reoriented inside a shallow container and airborne wrist rotations slip, drift, or exceed the reachable workspace\\n- [Translate a weak hooked grasp with surface support before lifting over thresholds.](global/support-guided-weak-hook-translation.md) \\u2014 A narrow rim or handle hook survives gentle motion but slips under airborne rotation or long carry.\\n- [Re-check duplicate identity before tuning a non-firing placement.](global/verify-duplicate-semantics.md)   "content": "---\nid: sam3-brand-noun-can-collision\nscope: global\nkind: perception\ntitle: Disambiguate look-alike cans by RGB label, never by SAM3 brand-noun prompt\napplies_when: two or more similar cans/cylinders are present and the task names one\n  by brand (\"tomato sauce\", \"alphabet soup\", a soup can)\nsymptom:\n- same box for different prompts\n- two cans\n- wrong can grabbed\n- sam3 low score\n- brand noun\n- tomato sauce\n- alphabet soup\nevidence:\n  cells:\n  - 10_task_t0_s0\n  attempts: 1\nconfidence: single-shot\nrelated: []\n---\n\n\nSAM3 cannot reliably ground grocery BRAND nouns; when two similar cans are\npresent, prompts for two different brands often return the SAME can.\n\n**Why:** SAM3's text grounding keys on coarse shape/appearance, not on printed\nbrand text. Both \"tomato sauce can\" and \"alphabet soup can\" matched the single\nmost can-like object (highest objectness), regardless of which brand was on it.\nThe score was even high (0.77-0.90) for the wrong prompt, so score is not a guard.\n\n**How to apply:** Decide can identity in the agentview hi-res RGB by LABEL colour\nand imagery, not by the SAM3 label: e.g. tomato_sauce = red/tomato label,\nalphabet_soup = blue label. Use SAM3 only to get a pixel/box for a can you have\nALREADY identified by eye. Confirm again in the wrist cam (label rim colour)\nbefore pi0_pick. Do not let the wrist re-identify freely \u2014 it locks onto\nlook-alikes too.\n\n**Falsify:** If, on a scene with two brand-labeled cans, distinct brand prompts\nreliably return distinct cans with the correct one top-ranked, this no longer\nholds.\n\n**Related:** [[pi0-pick-may-place-at-trained-left]]\n"
